id=\"offerings_update_form_83912_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eTraditional pumped hydro storage depends on high elevations - in other words, mountains. But suitable mountain sites are limited (and not exactly easy to build on).\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThat’s where \u003cb\u003eour High-Density Hydro (HD Hydro)\u003c/b\u003e changes the game.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBecause our system uses a fluid that’s \u003cb\u003e2.5× denser than water\u003c/b\u003e, we can achieve the same energy storage potential at \u003cb\u003e2.5× lower elevation\u003c/b\u003e. That means we’re no longer constrained to mountains; \u003cb\u003ehills now become viable sites\u003c/b\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe result?\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eA dramatic expansion of potential locations for long-duration energy storage\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eCloser proximity to demand centers\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eFaster progress toward a decarbonized grid\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe’re redefining what’s possible in renewable energy storage, one hill at a time.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan class=\"fr-video fr-dvb name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_83768_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eYves Rannou, former President \u0026amp; CEO of Alstom Wind and of GE Hydro amongst others, has joined the board\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;as a non-executive Director\u003c/span\u003e of RheEnergise, the UK company that is developing a new and advanced form of long duration hydro-energy storage with its proprietary High-Density Hydro® solution.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eWorking with RheEnergise’s senior management team led by CEO Stephen Crosher, and with the RheEnergise board chaired by Jim Campion, Yves Rannou will provide valuable domain expertise, experience and guidance on the commercialisation of the company’s long duration energy storage technology, its project development and its business strategy.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eYves\u0026nbsp;Rannou, said: “My experience at GE, Alstom, and other major industrial leaders such as Suez showed me a simple truth: standardization of large-scale industrial products is the path to lower costs, greater efficiency, and the most affordable energy solutions.\u0026nbsp;I've\u0026nbsp;long believed the first company to apply this model to hydropower would revolutionize the energy transition. RheEnergise is that company. They are not only standardizing system design, but their innovative, ultra-high-density fluid dramatically expands the range of\u0026nbsp;viable\u0026nbsp;storage sites and significantly reduces the\u0026nbsp;project\u0026nbsp;footprint. RheEnergise is finally unlocking the true scaling potential of pumped hydro.”\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='s-marginTop1_5 s-marginPullHoriz1 What is it and Why is it Important?\" name=\"offerings_update[headline]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_82941_offerings_update_headline\" /\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='js-headline_error u-colorRed s-marginPullTop0_5 s-marginBottom1 s-marginLeft0_5 u-hidden'\u003e\nEntry must be under 140 characters.\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='offerings-updates-shared-form__body-wrapper'\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"c-input-group\"\u003e\u003ctextarea class=\"c-input--textarea c-input c-input--required js-offering_update_body\" required=\"required\" data-show-video-autoplay-button=\"false\" data-file-formats=\"application/msword application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document application/pdf application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation\" aria-required=\"true\" placeholder=\"Description\" name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_82941_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eCurtailing excess energy is unfortunately costly. But this is not because renewables are expensive, or ineffective energy producers.\u0026nbsp;Its because the infrastructure to store the excess energy just doesn’t exist on the scale that its needed.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eIn California last year, where energy generation is far outpacing installed capacity, over 3,400GW of energy was curtailed. And this isn't just a California problem. This year in the UK already (Jan - May), curtailment has cost the GB electricity system £483.7m. That’s 42% higher than the figure for the same period in 2024 (£340.4m). This energy wasted represents a huge opportunity for Long Duration Energy Storage solutions.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003cspan\u003eEnergy storage solutions, especially long duration energy storage solutions like our HD Hydro, are the key to keeping the cost of curtailment, and the levels of energy wasted, down. With strong and reliable energy storage, any excess energy can be stored to use for times when energy demand is high, or when generation is low, to guarantee the cost of renewable energy stays low. Integrating storage solutions on the grid means reliable power, cheaper energy bills, and a profitable, renewable, decarbonised grid.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eSource:\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003ca name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_82467_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThe energy infrastructure market is rapidly expanding as countries grow their power grids and shift to renewables. This creates a huge need for long-duration energy storage (LDES) to balance supply and demand, stabilize grids, and meet decarbonization goals. The Long Duration Energy Storage Council states that LDES must scale 50 times faster than before to reach global energy targets—revealing a massive market gap and opportunity.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eGlobally, nearly 200 countries plan to triple renewable energy capacity by 2030. But because wind and solar are intermittent, flexible storage is vital to manage this variability, keep costs low, and ensure grid stability. In the U.S., demand for utility-scale storage is soaring due to renewables growth, industrial decarbonization, and data centers—projected to need 40–50 times more storage than currently installed.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eRheEnergise’s patented HD Hydro® is positioned to lead this transformation. The LDES market could exceed US$4 trillion by 2040, with strong commercial traction already—RheEnergise’s pipeline is worth over US$600 million. Major markets in North America, Europe, and Asia are setting ambitious decarbonization targets, driving demand for reliable, scalable storage solutions.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eUnlike battery storage, HD Hydro® keeps most of its project value within local economies, avoiding import tariffs and retaining jobs. It’s also 40–50% cheaper than lithium-ion batteries for storage over 8 hours, providing an affordable, domestic solution to stabilize grids, lower peak costs, and prevent blackouts.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith growing global momentum and a proven, cost-competitive technology, RheEnergise is well-placed to capture significant market share in the fast-growing LDES sector—helping power systems worldwide transition to reliable, low-carbon energy.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_81998_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eHow does RheEnergise stack up?\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eTo compare energy storage solutions, we use Levelised Cost of Storage (LCOS) — factoring in energy costs, discount rates, cycles per day, inflation, and project lifespan. It’s the fairest way we believe to see what delivers the best value.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eOur verdict on the competition based on the above methodology:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eLi-ion Batteries: Too expensive for long-duration storage. Supply chains are vulnerable to tariffs and geopolitics, lifespan is short (10–12 years), and recycling poses environmental challenges.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eFlow Batteries: Dependent on costly, scarce vanadium, with limited long-term reliability and similar recycling concerns.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eOther Gravity Systems: Lifting blocks and similar tech need new structures, lots of concrete, and deliver limited efficiency — driving up costs and carbon footprints.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eCompressed Air: Site-limited, slow to build, risky, and only 40–50% efficient.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eThermal Storage: High heat loss means low efficiency — not viable for long-duration needs yet.\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe RheEnergise edge:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eOur High-Density Hydro (HD Hydro) delivers sustainable, cost-effective, efficient (80% round-trip) and scalable long-duration storage — without the supply chain headaches or heavy footprint.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.com/p/images/attachments/original/000/198/215/198215-1757002404-6d0205e471b2b69edc52ed4c8f7a266496571a0f.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic id=\"offerings_update_form_81629_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eOur fluid is so dense, concrete floats!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e2.5x denser than water is quite hard to visualize. But our IP protected fluid is so dense, concrete would float to the top.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThis innovation completely breaks the paradigm of pumped hydro storage. The biggest problem with traditional pumped hydro is that there just aren’t enough sites that are viable to make it work. By changing the fluid density, we can reduce the elevation and footprint of a site, unlocking hundreds of thousands of potential sites for our HD Hydro. In fact, there are nearly 300,000 potential sites in North America alone!\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eJoin our founders for a \u003ca href=\"https://www.eventbrite.co.uk/e/the-energy-game-changers-a-deep-dive-with-rheenergise-founders-tickets-1433149953089?aff=CampaignUpdates\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eQ\u0026amp;A session\u003c/a\u003e to learn more about how we can revolutionize energy in North America. name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_81047_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eCalifornia wants to lead the world on supporting climate-friendly solutions. And for the most part, it has. Rooftop solar, innovation incentives—California was first out of the gate on many fronts. But lately, a troubling pattern has emerged: the state is generating more clean electricity than it uses, then paying the price—literally—for not being able to store it.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAt times, vast amounts of California solar are curtailed, wasted simply because the grid can’t move it around or absorb it all. Then, when the sun sets and demand surges, the state scrambles to import power or fire up fossil fuel plants. This bottleneck is costing Californians—in both dollars and emissions.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe solution isn’t some futuristic breakthrough still on the whiteboard. It’s already here. It’s low-tech, proven, and now reimagined for California’s climate, topography, and water constraints: RheEnergise’s High-Density Hydro pumped energy storage.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith over 20,000 Potential sites identified across California, RheEnergise’s HD Hydro solution is positioned to lead. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eLong-duration storage gives California something it desperately needs: resilience. With wildfires, heatwaves, and transmission stress threatening blackouts more frequently, having days of energy stored on-site—not just hours—could be the difference between lights on or off, homes safe or evacuated.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFrom the outside, it looks like everything is lining up: the ambition, the need, the opportunity.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe only thing missing is the action. Join us today to accelerate California towards a secure and reliable renewable energy grid. id=\"offerings_update_form_80944_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eBig policy changes are making waves, but\u0026nbsp;we’re\u0026nbsp;staying steady.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eLast month’s passage of the One Big, Beautiful Bill (OBBB) brought plenty of headlines and plenty of questions for the renewables industry. But here’s the good news: \u003cstrong\u003epumped hydro energy storage is still on solid ground\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTax credit support for pumped hydro storage solutions like our High-Density Hydro® remains fully in place through 2033, with a gradual phase-out through 2036.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;That’s a positive signal of long-term confidence, and a longer runway to scale, attract capital, and\u003cstrong\u003e\u0026nbsp;keep the grid stable when it matters most.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eAs the need for resilient, long-duration energy storage grows,\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003eclosed-loop pumped hydro stands out\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e: low-impact, highly flexible, and now more innovative than ever name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_80457_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eHello and welcome!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe’re thrilled to invite you to be part of a unique opportunity to invest in RheEnergise, an energy storage company built on innovation, scalability, and a clear vision for the future. Our long duration energy storage solution is modelled off traditional pumped hydro storage (think dams), but on a greatly reduced scale. We achieve this using a working fluid that is 2.5x denser than water, without compromising efficiency or cost. We can operate on hills, NOT mountains, unlocking tens of thousands more sites for hydropower worldwide. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eYour participation helps us drive our mission: To provide affordable, accessible, clean energy for everyone, everywhere, by enabling integration of more renewables and eliminating the need for fossil fuels. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhy This Opportunity Stands Out: \u0026nbsp;\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eA Massive Market Opportunity\u003c/strong\u003e: We’re addressing a global market valued in trillions of dollars, driven by both growing energy demand, and the transition to low-carbon energy solutions. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStrong Commercial Traction\u003c/strong\u003e: Our solution is gaining significant global interest, thanks to our clear competitive advantage in both performance and cost. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePrivate Investor \u0026amp; Government-Backed Innovation\u003c/strong\u003e: We have raised ~CAD$30m in funding, of which over 2/3 is from government grants and 1/3 from previous investment rounds, strongly validating both the technical innovation and the impact of our technology. \u0026nbsp;\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePatented Proven Technology, De-Risked Through Testing\u003c/strong\u003e: Our highly skilled engineering and chemistry teams, based in Canada, have carried out extensive physical testing to rigorously validate the system and de-risk the solution. This, in turn, provides confidence for commercial deployment at scale.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe're not just raising capital—we're building a community of visionary backers who share our ambition and understand the value of getting in early. As we scale, we’re committed to delivering transparency, regular updates, and strategic execution aligned with your expectations. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe encourage you to explore the full offering details and financials right here on our Republic campaign page. Our leadership team is also available to contact directly should you have questions or wish to discuss the opportunity further, such as reviewing a campaign deck. Please just ask.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIf you wish to hear directly from us about our offering, we will be hosting a series of Q\u0026amp;A sessions online. Find out dates and sign up here: \u003ca href=\"https://www.eventbrite.co.uk/e/the-energy-game-changers-a-deep-dive-with-rheenergise-founders-tickets-1433149953089?aff=RepublicCUpdate\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eWEBINAR LINK\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThank you for considering this investment.
